# CSS text, font and writing properties required or referenced by SVG 2 text

Part of the [W3C specification audit](../design_docs/0071-w3c_spec_audit.md). One row per element,
attribute, property, value and feature in scope. Findings come from reading Donner source at
revision `b39a524a2` and the specification versions listed below. No code was built or executed
for this ledger, so every behavioral claim is a code-reading result until a test confirms it. All
paths are relative to the repository root.


Donner evidence is cited as `path:line` relative to the checkout root at revision `b39a524a2`.
Every behavioral statement is a code-reading result ("by code reading"); nothing was built or
run. Donner has two text backends that share one layout engine (`donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc`):
the basic backend (`TextBackendSimple`, stb_truetype, no OpenType layout) and the HarfBuzz and
FreeType backend (`TextBackendFull`). A row describes both unless it says "simple:" or "full:".

Two facts shape most rows and are stated once here:

- The basic backend requires trusted font data (`donner/svg/text/TextBackendSimple.h:24`), so
  every document-supplied `@font-face` font is replaced by the embedded fallback face before
  shaping (`donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:244-250`). Font rows that say "document fonts" apply
  to the full backend only.
- A CSS declaration for an unknown property is dropped with a diagnostic
  (`donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:3434-3466`), which is the correct CSS error
  handling; a "missing" property therefore fails safely.

Layout algorithm steps of the SVG text chapter itself (chunks, anchoring, `inline-size`
wrapping order, text on a path, `textLength`) belong to `text_layout.md` and are referenced, not
audited, here. `text-anchor`, `text-overflow`, `text-rendering` and `inline-size` are SVG or
CSS Overflow properties and are outside this ledger.

## Inventory

Derivation:

- Properties: every entry of the property index of CSS Fonts 4, CSS Text 3, CSS Text Decoration
  3, CSS Writing Modes 4 (a superset of Level 3: it adds `sideways-rl`, `sideways-lr` and the
  `digits` form of `text-combine-upright`) and CSS Inline Layout 3, extracted from the
  pages. Each gets a `prop:` row. `font-stretch` and `word-wrap` are legacy aliases with their
  own rows.
- Values: for every property that can apply to SVG text, every keyword, function and grammar
  alternative in the property definition table and its value definitions (`dfn` entries of type
  `value` in the pages), plus the legacy SVG 1.1 keywords Donner still accepts.
  Properties classified `not-applicable` have only a `prop:` row, except `baseline-source` and
  `font-display`, whose few values are listed for completeness.
- Features: the `@font-face`, `@font-feature-values` and `@font-palette-values` rules with every
  descriptor, format() and tech() keyword; the font matching algorithm step by step, cluster
  matching, fallback and synthesis; white space processing and line breaking rules of CSS Text
  3; decoration propagation; vertical typesetting; baseline metrics; the rules of UAX #9 as CSS
  applies them; the WOFF 1.0 and 2.0 user agent conformance rules; and one row per font format
  or color font table type.

Out of scope for an SVG user agent without script, with the reason:

- CSS Fonts 4 sections 12 and 13 (CSSOM interfaces, serialization) and the font display
  timeline (section 3 and the `font-display` descriptor): no script and no progressive
  rendering. `font-size: math`: MathML only.
- CSS Inline Layout 3 `text-box*`, `inline-sizing`, `initial-letter*` and `baseline-source`:
  CSS box-model layout with no counterpart in SVG text.
- CSS Writing Modes 4 sections 6 to 8 (abstract box layout, orthogonal flows, principal
  writing mode, page direction): CSS box layout.
- CSS Text 3 text-align and justification are NOT out of scope: SVG 2 styling 6.7 lists
  `text-align` and `text-align-last` as required properties, so they have rows.
- `size-adjust` is a CSS Fonts 5 descriptor; it has a row only for completeness.

### CSS Fonts 4: `@font-face` rule and descriptors

| Key | Class | Spec anchor | Requirement (paraphrase) | Parse evidence | Consume evidence | Tests | Impact | Size | Notes |

| `feat:css-fonts-4/matching-step-family` | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-matching-algorithm | For each family in the list: resolve generics, then look the name up case-insensitively among `@font-face` families first and installed families second; an undefined family is skipped. | donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:2225-2247 | donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:229-240, donner/svg/resources/FontManager.cc:578-592 (hasFamily), :636-650 (`@font-face` first), :689-694 (provider second) | FontSelection.FamilyListSkipsAnUnknownFamily; StylesheetParser/FontFace identity tests (FontFaceIdentityKeyTest.FamilyNameIsCaseInsensitive) | high | - | Case folding is ASCII only (donner/svg/resources/FontManager.cc:57-66); the spec asks for Unicode default caseless matching, which matters only for non-ASCII family names. |
| `feat:css-fonts-4/matching-step-width` | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-style-matching | font-width narrows first: an exact or in-range face wins; otherwise for requests at or below 100 percent narrower faces are tried before wider, and the reverse above 100 percent. | donner/css/parser/StylesheetParser.cc:384-430 | donner/svg/resources/FontManager.cc:70-80, :112-119 | FontManagerTest.MatchingFiltersWidthBeforeStyle, .NormalWidthSearchPrefersNarrowerFace, .WidthPercentagesFollowDirectionalFallback | medium | - | - |
| `feat:css-fonts-4/matching-step-style` | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-style-matching | font-style narrows second: italic prefers italic, then oblique, then normal; oblique prefers oblique by nearest angle, then italic, then normal; normal prefers normal, then oblique, then italic. | donner/css/parser/StylesheetParser.cc:445-455 | donner/svg/resources/FontManager.cc:82-90 | FontManagerTest.ParsedDescriptorsSelectFacesInsteadOfDeclarationOrder; FontSelection.BoldItalicRequestSelectsAvailableItalicFaceBeforeUprightBold | low | S | The keyword order is right; there are no oblique angles or ranges, so angle-distance ordering does not exist. |
| `feat:css-fonts-4/matching-step-weight` | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-style-matching | font-weight narrows third: in-range wins; a request of 400 to 500 tries heavier up to 500, then lighter, then heavier; below 400 tries lighter then heavier; above 500 the reverse. | donner/css/parser/StylesheetParser.cc:330-382 | donner/svg/resources/FontManager.cc:92-110 | FontManagerTest.WeightFallbackFollowsCssSearchDirection, .OverlappingWeightRangesUseLatestMatchingDeclaration | medium | - | - |

Two differences: the search runs only for clusters whose primary glyphs came back as .notdef, with a bounded coverage-query budget (donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:3960-4003), and the 'system fallback' step is replaced by 'every other registered face in registration order' (donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:5188-5191). |

### WOFF 1.0 and WOFF 2.0 conformance rules

| Key | Class | Spec anchor | Requirement (paraphrase) | Parse evidence | Consume evidence | Tests | Impact | Size | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `feat:woff1/signature` | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2012/REC-WOFF-20121213/#conform-nomagicnumber-reject | A file whose signature is not 'wOFF' must be rejected. | donner/base/fonts/WoffParser.cc:150-154 | donner/svg/resources/FontManager.cc:959-961 | WoffParser.RejectsMalformedHeadersAndDirectories | low | - | - |
| `feat:woff1/length-and-bounds` | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2012/REC-WOFF-20121213/#conform-overlap-reject | Tables or blocks extending past the end of the file must be rejected. | donner/base/fonts/WoffParser.cc:186-193, :209-212 | n/a | WoffParser.RejectsInvalidTableRangesAndSizes | medium | - | - |
| `feat:woff1/zlib-decompress` | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2012/REC-WOFF-20121213/#conform-decompressfailure | Compressed tables must be decompressed with zlib; a failure or a size other than origLength invalidates the file. | donner/base/fonts/WoffParser.cc:232-244 (expected size passed to the inflater) | n/a | WoffParser.Simple; WoffParser.RejectsInvalidTableRangesAndSizes | medium | - | - |
| `feat:woff1/reserved-field` | missing | https://www.w3.org/TR/2012/REC-WOFF-20121213/#conform-reserved-reject | A non-zero reserved header field must cause rejection. | none: donner/base/fonts/WoffParser.cc:163 skips the reserved field without testing it | n/a | none | low | S | - |
| `feat:woff1/totalsfntsize` | missing | https://www.w3.org/TR/2012/REC-WOFF-20121213/#conform-totalsize-longword-reject | totalSfntSize must be a multiple of 4 and equal the reconstructed size, else reject. | none: donner/base/fonts/WoffParser.cc:164-168 uses it only as an upper bound | n/a | none | low | S | - |
| `feat:woff1/overlap-and-extraneous-data` | missing | https://www.w3.org/TR/2012/REC-WOFF-20121213/#conform-extraneous-reject | Overlapping table ranges, gaps, or bytes after the last block must cause rejection. | none: donner/base/fonts/WoffParser.cc:196-214 checks each table against the file size only | n/a | none | low | S | - |
| `feat:woff1/complength-gt-origlength` | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2012/REC-WOFF-20121213/#conform-compLength | An entry whose compLength exceeds origLength is invalid and must not be loaded. | donner/base/fonts/WoffParser.cc:232-241 (not tested directly; such a table is sent to the inflater, which is expected to fail) | n/a | none | low | S | Unverified: rejection depends on the inflate result. |
| `feat:woff1/metadata-and-private-blocks` | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2012/REC-WOFF-20121213/#conform-metadata-noeffect | Metadata and private blocks must not affect rendering; ranges that overlap or run past the file must cause rejection. | donner/base/fonts/WoffParser.cc:171-185 (offsets read and discarded) | n/a | none | low | S | No effect on rendering (conformant); the range checks are not made. |

### CSS Text 3: spacing

| Key | Class | Spec anchor | Requirement (paraphrase) | Parse evidence | Consume evidence | Tests | Impact | Size | Notes |

| `prop:letter-spacing` | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CRD-css-text-3-20260814/#propdef-letter-spacing | Extra spacing between typographic character units: `normal` or a length (may be negative); inherited; not applied at the start or end of a line; not applied between characters of cursive scripts; a non-zero value disables optional ligatures. Presentation attribute in SVG 2, where percentages were removed. | donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:838-849 (normal, or ParseLengthPercentage), registered :2850 | donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:936-942 (resolved to pixels per span), :7158-7162 (horizontal), :7037-7041 (vertical), :7201-7204 (bidi plan), :1272-1277 (text on a path) | SVGTextElementPublicApiTests.LetterSpacingAffectsCharacterPositions; PropertyRegistry.NegativeSpacingAndBaselineShiftRemainValid; resvg text/letter-spacing/* (12 cases; large-negative.svg marked undefined, Arabic cases full backend only) | medium | S | Applied after every cluster including the last one on the line, percentages are still accepted, and optional ligatures are not disabled. CSS declarations also accept a unitless number here (AllowUserUnits for all but four properties, donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:3446-3452); CSS requires a unit outside presentation attributes. |
| `val:letter-spacing/normal` | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CRD-css-text-3-20260814/#valdef-letter-spacing-normal | No additional spacing (computes to zero). | donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:842-844 | donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:936-942 (resolved to pixels per span), :7158-7162 (horizontal), :7037-7041 (vertical), :7201-7204 (bidi plan), :1272-1277 (text on a path) | resvg text/letter-spacing/normal.svg, 0.svg | low | - | - |
| `val:letter-spacing/<length>` | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CRD-css-text-3-20260814/#valdef-letter-spacing-length | Additional space per typographic character unit, half on each side, omitted at line start and end; cursive scripts are not spaced; optional ligatures are disabled when non-zero. | donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:838-849 | donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:7211 and :7168 (full amount added after each cluster; cursive clusters skipped via donner/svg/text/TextBackendFull.cc:50-75, :901-903) | resvg text/letter-spacing/3.svg, -3.svg, 1mm.svg, mixed-spacing.svg, on-Arabic.svg (full backend); TextEngineScriptedTest.OverflowWrapGreedyMetricsIncludeLetterSpacing | medium | S | By code reading the trailing space after the last cluster is kept, so `text-anchor: middle` and `end` are offset by half or all of one letter-spacing compared with CSS Text 3 (this is the SVG 1.1 and resvg behavior). No ligature suppression: `fi` stays ligated under letter-spacing in the full backend. |

### CSS Text 4 white-space longhands named by SVG 2 (styling 6.7 cites css-text-4 for white-space; the UA style sheet uses white-space-collapse)

| `feat:svg2/xml-space-preserve-ua-rule` | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/styling.html#UAStyleSheet | SVG 2 UA style sheet: `*[xml\|space=preserve] { white-space-collapse: preserve-spaces; }`, that is, sequences of white space are kept and tabs and segment breaks become spaces; an authored white-space wins by the cascade. | n/a (no UA style sheet rule; the mapping is hard-coded) | donner/svg/components/text/TextSystem.cc:97-105 (tabs, newlines and carriage returns become spaces, nothing collapses), :593-598 (applies only when white-space is not specified) | TextSystemTest.XmlSpacePreserveKeepsWhitespaceAndInheritance, .CssWhiteSpaceCascadeOverridesLegacySpace | medium | - | - |
| `prop:white-space-collapse` | missing | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-text-4-20260814/#propdef-white-space-collapse | Longhand of white-space controlling collapsing: collapse, discard, preserve, preserve-breaks, preserve-spaces, break-spaces; inherited. | absent: `grep -rlF "white-space-collapse" donner/` (all file types) matches no source file | none (only the white-space shorthand keywords exist) | none | low | M | The behaviors of collapse, preserve, preserve-breaks and break-spaces are reachable through the white-space keywords, and preserve-spaces through xml:space; only the property name and `discard` are absent. white-space would have to become a real shorthand. |

### CSS Writing Modes 4: direction and unicode-bidi

| Key | Class | Spec anchor | Requirement (paraphrase) | Parse evidence | Consume evidence | Tests | Impact | Size | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `prop:direction` | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#propdef-direction | Base inline direction of a box: sets the bidi paragraph embedding level, the meaning of start and end, and (with unicode-bidi) embedding direction for inlines; inherited. Presentation attribute in SVG 2, where it also selects the text-anchor reference edge. | donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:146-161, registered :2776 | donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:393 and :4372-4379 (paragraph base from the text element), :842-852 and :891-894 (per-span anchor edge) | PropertyRegistry.ParsesDirectionAndUnicodeBidi; TextEngineTest.BidiRtlParagraphSendsTrailingPunctuationToTheLineStart; SVGTextElementPublicApiTests.RtlDirectionMirrorsStartAndEndAnchors; resvg text/direction/rtl.svg (compared on the full-text tier), rtl-with-vertical-writing-mode.svg (undefined-behavior marker) | high | M | Works for horizontal text inside the reordering scope (see `feat:uax9/application-scope`: not with inline-size wrapping, on a path, or with preserved newlines or tabs). `rtl` is ignored for vertical writing modes and on text paths (donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:840-844, :4372-4376), and a `direction` on a tspan only moves the anchor edge because embeddings are not implemented. |
| `val:direction/ltr` | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#valdef-direction-ltr | Left-to-right base direction (embedding level 0). | donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:149-150 | donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:4377 | BidiParagraphTest.DefaultSvgBaseIsExplicitLeftToRight; TextEngineTest.BidiNormalArabicFirstDoesNotReverseTheLatinRunOrFinalPunctuation | medium | - | - |
| `val:direction/rtl` | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#valdef-direction-rtl | Right-to-left base direction (embedding level 1); start is the right edge. | donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:151-152 | donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:4372-4379, :842-852; donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:4453-4481 (ordinaryScope: the paragraph reordering plan is skipped when inline-size is greater than 0, when any span is on a path, when any span preserves segment breaks and contains a newline or tab, in a vertical writing mode with an upright span, and in a vertical writing mode where a non-negative textLength is set on the text element, a span or a managed length owner) | TextEngineTest.BidiRtlParagraphSendsTrailingPunctuationToTheLineStart; TextEngineScriptedTest.OverflowWrapGreedilyPacksUnevenRtlWords; resvg text/direction/rtl.svg (compared on the full-text tier) | high | M | Paragraph level and anchor mirroring are right for horizontal text inside the reordering scope (see `feat:uax9/application-scope`). In text wrapped by inline-size the base direction reaches the wrapping code but UAX #9 reordering does not run; in every vertical writing mode and on a path the base direction is ignored. |
| `prop:unicode-bidi` | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#propdef-unicode-bidi | How an element takes part in the bidi algorithm: normal, embed, isolate, bidi-override, isolate-override, plaintext; not inherited. Presentation attribute in SVG 2. | donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:163-186 (all six keywords), registered :2785; not inherited (donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.h:434-435) | donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:394 (only `OverridesBidi` of the text element's own value is read; donner/svg/core/UnicodeBidi.h:35-37) | PropertyRegistry.ParsesDirectionAndUnicodeBidi; SVGTextElementPublicApiTests.BidiOverrideAppliesOnlyToTheTextElementItself (asserts that a tspan value is ignored); resvg text/unicode-bidi/bidi-override.svg (skipped, full backend only) | medium | L | Parse is complete. Consumption is limited to an override on the text element; values on tspan and textPath, and embed, isolate and plaintext anywhere, do nothing. |
| `val:unicode-bidi/normal` | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#valdef-unicode-bidi-normal | No additional embedding level; implicit reordering works across the element boundary. | donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:168 | donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:4536-4541 | TextEngineTest.BidiNormalLatinAndArabicUseSeparateRunDirections; TextBidiConvergence.LatinArabicAndPunctuationKeepTheirOwnDirectionsWithSpacing | medium | - | - |
| `val:unicode-bidi/embed` | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#valdef-unicode-bidi-embed | On an inline, opens an embedding in the element's direction (as LRE/RLE ... PDF). SVG 2 text 11.10.2.4 uses exactly this in its example. | donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:169 | none: parsed and stored, no reader (`grep -rn unicodeBidi donner/svg --include=*.cc` finds only donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:394 and a pass-through at donner/svg/renderer/RendererDriver.cc:1819) | PropertyRegistry.ParsesDirectionAndUnicodeBidi (parse only) | medium | M | Parse-only. The SVG 2 chapter's own MAC-address example (`tspan direction=ltr unicode-bidi=embed` inside rtl text) renders without the embedding. |
| `val:unicode-bidi/isolate` | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#valdef-unicode-bidi-isolate | On an inline, isolates its content (as LRI/RLI ... PDI) so it reorders as one neutral. | donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:170 | none (same grep as embed) | PropertyRegistry.ParsesDirectionAndUnicodeBidi (parse only) | medium | M | Parse-only. |
| `val:unicode-bidi/bidi-override` | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#valdef-unicode-bidi-bidi-override | Overrides implicit character directions with the element's direction; on a block container it applies to the whole paragraph, on an inline it opens an override (LRO/RLO ... PDF). | donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:171 | donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:394, :4387-4389 (text element only), donner/svg/text/BidiParagraph.cc:155-166 (override levels) | TextEngineTest.BidiOverrideReversesEveryCharacterOfAnRtlParagraph; BidiParagraphTest.OverrideGivesEveryScalarTheBaseLevel; resvg text/unicode-bidi/bidi-override.svg (compares, full text) | medium | M | Honored on the text element, ignored on tspan and textPath. |
| `val:unicode-bidi/isolate-override` | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#valdef-unicode-bidi-isolate-override | Isolate plus override. | donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:172 | donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:394 (treated like bidi-override on the text element; the isolation half is moot there) | PropertyRegistry.ParsesDirectionAndUnicodeBidi; no layout test uses this keyword | low | M | Same limits as bidi-override. |

### UAX #9 bidirectional algorithm as applied by CSS

| Key | Class | Spec anchor | Requirement (paraphrase) | Parse evidence | Consume evidence | Tests | Impact | Size | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `feat:uax9/application-scope` | partial | https://www.unicode.org/reports/tr9/tr9-52.html#Bidi_Conformance_Testing | Every line of text is reordered by the algorithm: one paragraph per block container (for SVG, per text element), per line after line breaking. | n/a | donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:4453-4481 (ordinaryScope: the paragraph reordering plan is skipped when inline-size is greater than 0, when any span is on a path, when any span preserves segment breaks and contains a newline or tab, in a vertical writing mode with an upright span, and in a vertical writing mode where a non-negative textLength is set on the text element, a span or a managed length owner) | TextEngineTest.BidiNormalInlineSizeKeepsTheExistingSimpleShapingScope and .BidiNormalExcludedScopesKeepControlsOutOfPositionListIndices (both assert the exclusion); resvg text/writing-mode/mixed-languages-with-tb-and-underline.svg (render-only, undefined behavior) | high | L | Reordered: horizontal text with no inline-size, no path and no preserved newline or tab, including multi-chunk text positioned by x/y lists; and vertical text with mixed or sideways orientation and no textLength. The excluded layouts get no UAX #9 reordering: the full backend then relies on HarfBuzz's per-run direction guess; the simple backend draws logical order. Separately, the paragraph base direction is ignored in every vertical writing mode and on a path (donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:842-844, :4372-4379). The same scope is recorded in `text_layout.md` (`feat:svg2/text-bidi-scope-all-layouts`). |
| `feat:uax9/P1-paragraph-separation` | conformant | https://www.unicode.org/reports/tr9/tr9-52.html#P1 | Text is split into paragraphs at paragraph separators; each is processed independently. | n/a | donner/svg/text/BidiParagraph.cc:136-150 (one SheenBidi paragraph per separator-delimited range) | BidiParagraphTest.ParagraphSeparatorsAndCrLfDoNotDropFollowingText | low | - | - |
| `feat:uax9/P2-P3-first-strong` | partial | https://www.unicode.org/reports/tr9/tr9-52.html#P2 | Without a higher-level override the paragraph level comes from the first strong character, skipping isolates. CSS uses this only for unicode-bidi: plaintext. | n/a | donner/svg/text/BidiParagraph.cc:136-138 (base 2 requests detection); never requested by layout (donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:4540) | BidiParagraphConformance.Unicode17Characters and .Unicode17BidiClasses (the Unicode BidiCharacterTest and BidiTest data) exercise the auto base; no layout test | low | S | The primitive exists and is tested; nothing connects it to plaintext. |
| `feat:uax9/HL1-paragraph-level-from-direction` | conformant | https://www.unicode.org/reports/tr9/tr9-52.html#HL1 | A higher-level protocol may set the paragraph level; CSS sets it from `direction` on the block container. | n/a | donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:4372-4379, :4540 | BidiParagraphTest.RightToLeftBaseMovesTrailingNeutralsToTheLineStart; TextEngineTest.BidiRtlParagraphSendsTrailingPunctuationToTheLineStart | medium | - | - |

## Tests to create

One bullet per row with no test anywhere (not-applicable and legacy rows excluded). Rows of one missing property share a parser, so one file per property with one case per value is enough.

- `val:font/caption` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#valdef-font-caption): <text style="font: caption">: declaration must be valid and replace an earlier font-size
- `val:font/icon` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#valdef-font-icon): <text style="font: icon">: declaration must be valid and replace an earlier font-size
- `val:font/menu` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#valdef-font-menu): <text style="font: menu">: declaration must be valid and replace an earlier font-size
- `val:font/message-box` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#valdef-font-message-box): <text style="font: message-box">: declaration must be valid and replace an earlier font-size
- `val:font/small-caption` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#valdef-font-small-caption): <text style="font: small-caption">: declaration must be valid and replace an earlier font-size
- `val:font/status-bar` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#valdef-font-status-bar): <text style="font: status-bar">: declaration must be valid and replace an earlier font-size
- `val:font-family/escaped-identifier` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-family-name-syntax): font-family: Noto\ Sans (escaped space) must select the same face as 'Noto Sans'
- `val:font-family/system-ui` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#generic-font-families): <text font-family="system-ui, serif"> with a host mapping absent: must still render text
- `val:font-family/math` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#generic-font-families): <text font-family="math, serif"> with a host mapping absent: must still render text
- `val:font-family/ui-serif` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#generic-font-families): <text font-family="ui-serif, serif"> with a host mapping absent: must still render text
- `val:font-family/ui-sans-serif` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#generic-font-families): <text font-family="ui-sans-serif, serif"> with a host mapping absent: must still render text
- `val:font-family/ui-monospace` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#generic-font-families): <text font-family="ui-monospace, serif"> with a host mapping absent: must still render text
- `val:font-family/ui-rounded` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#generic-font-families): <text font-family="ui-rounded, serif"> with a host mapping absent: must still render text
- `val:font-family/generic(fangsong)` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#typedef-generic-font-script-specific): font-family: generic(fangsong), serif must parse and fall through to serif
- `val:font-family/generic(kai)` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#typedef-generic-font-script-specific): font-family: generic(kai), serif must parse and fall through to serif
- `val:font-family/generic(khmer-mul)` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#typedef-generic-font-script-specific): font-family: generic(khmer-mul), serif must parse and fall through to serif
- `val:font-family/generic(nastaliq)` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#typedef-generic-font-script-specific): font-family: generic(nastaliq), serif must parse and fall through to serif
- `val:font-family/initial` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#propdef-font-family): text without any font-family: assert the face chosen equals the serif mapping
- `prop:font-width` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#propdef-font-width): <text style="font-width: condensed"> must select the same face as font-stretch: condensed
- `val:font-width/<percentage [0,inf]>` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#valdef-font-width-percentage-0): font-stretch: 87.5% must match a face declared with font-width: 87.5%
- `val:font-style/left` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#valdef-font-style-left): font-style: left must be valid and select an italic face
- `val:font-style/right` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#valdef-font-style-right): font-style: right must be valid
- `val:font-style/oblique <angle>` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#valdef-font-style-oblique-angle--90deg-90deg): font-style: oblique 20deg must be valid and slant more than oblique 5deg
- `val:font-size/xxx-large` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#absolute-size-mapping): font-size: xxx-large must compute to three times medium
- `prop:font-synthesis` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#propdef-font-synthesis): font-synthesis: none must disable synthetic oblique and synthetic small caps
- `val:font-synthesis/none` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#propdef-font-synthesis): font-synthesis: none parses and maps to the longhands
- `val:font-synthesis/weight` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#propdef-font-synthesis): font-synthesis: weight parses and maps to the longhands
- `val:font-synthesis/style` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#propdef-font-synthesis): font-synthesis: style parses and maps to the longhands
- `val:font-synthesis/small-caps` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#propdef-font-synthesis): font-synthesis: small-caps parses and maps to the longhands
- `val:font-synthesis/position` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#propdef-font-synthesis): font-synthesis: position parses and maps to the longhands
- `prop:font-synthesis-weight` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#propdef-font-synthesis-weight): bold text in a regular-only host face with font-synthesis-weight: none must not embolden
- `val:font-synthesis-weight/auto` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#valdef-font-synthesis-weight-auto): <text style="font-synthesis-weight: auto"> compared with a reference
- `val:font-synthesis-weight/none` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#valdef-font-synthesis-weight-none): <text style="font-synthesis-weight: none"> compared with a reference
- `prop:font-synthesis-position` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#propdef-font-synthesis-position): font-variant-position: super with font-synthesis-position: none on a face without sups
- `val:font-synthesis-position/auto` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#valdef-font-synthesis-position-auto): <text style="font-synthesis-position: auto"> compared with a reference
- `val:font-synthesis-position/none` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#valdef-font-synthesis-position-none): <text style="font-synthesis-position: none"> compared with a reference
- `val:font-variant/none` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-variant-none-value): font-variant: none must disable the fi ligature in a face that has one
- `val:font-variant/<longhand-combinations>` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#propdef-font-variant): font-variant: small-caps oldstyle-nums must apply both features
- `prop:font-variant-ligatures` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#propdef-font-variant-ligatures): <text style="font-variant-ligatures: none"> compared with the default rendering in a feature-rich face
- `val:font-variant-ligatures/normal` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-variant-ligatures-normal-value): <text style="font-variant-ligatures: normal">

- `val:font-variant-ligatures/discretionary-ligatures` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#valdef-font-variant-ligatures-discretionary-ligatures): <text style="font-variant-ligatures: discretionary-ligatures">

- `prop:font-variant-position` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#propdef-font-variant-position): <text style="font-variant-position: sub"> compared with the default rendering in a feature-rich face

- `feat:css-fonts-4/font-face-font-feature-settings` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-rend-desc): `@font-face` with font-feature-settings and a visible effect on line or glyph metrics
- `feat:css-fonts-4/font-face-font-variation-settings` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-rend-desc): `@font-face` with font-variation-settings and a visible effect on line or glyph metrics
- `feat:css-fonts-4/font-face-font-named-instance` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-named-instance): `@font-face` with font-named-instance and a visible effect on line or glyph metrics
- `feat:css-fonts-4/font-face-font-language-override` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-language-override-desc): `@font-face` with font-language-override and a visible effect on line or glyph metrics
- `feat:css-fonts-4/font-face-ascent-override` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-metrics-override-desc): `@font-face` with ascent-override and a visible effect on line or glyph metrics
- `feat:css-fonts-4/font-face-descent-override` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-metrics-override-desc): `@font-face` with descent-override and a visible effect on line or glyph metrics
- `feat:css-fonts-4/font-face-line-gap-override` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-metrics-override-desc): `@font-face` with line-gap-override and a visible effect on line or glyph metrics
- `feat:css-fonts-4/@font-feature-values` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-feature-values): `@font-feature-values` Family { `@styleset` { alt: 2; } } with font-variant-alternates: styleset(alt)
- `feat:css-fonts-4/@font-feature-values/@stylistic` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#at-ruledef-font-feature-values-stylistic): minimal `<text>` case exercising: Feature value block `@stylistic` mapping names to feature indexes.
- `feat:css-fonts-4/@font-feature-values/@historical-forms` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#at-ruledef-font-feature-values-historical-forms): minimal `<text>` case exercising: Feature value block `@historical-forms` mapping names to feature indexes.
- `feat:css-fonts-4/@font-feature-values/@styleset` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#at-ruledef-font-feature-values-styleset): minimal `<text>` case exercising: Feature value block `@styleset` mapping names to feature indexes.
- `feat:css-fonts-4/@font-feature-values/@character-variant` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#at-ruledef-font-feature-values-character-variant): minimal `<text>` case exercising: Feature value block `@character-variant` mapping names to feature indexes.
- `feat:css-fonts-4/@font-feature-values/@swash` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#at-ruledef-font-feature-values-swash): minimal `<text>` case exercising: Feature value block `@swash` mapping names to feature indexes.
- `feat:css-fonts-4/@font-feature-values/@ornaments` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#at-ruledef-font-feature-values-ornaments): minimal `<text>` case exercising: Feature value block `@ornaments` mapping names to feature indexes.
- `feat:css-fonts-4/@font-feature-values/@annotation` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#at-ruledef-font-feature-values-annotation): minimal `<text>` case exercising: Feature value block `@annotation` mapping names to feature indexes.
- `feat:css-fonts-4/@font-palette-values` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#font-palette-values): `@font-palette-values` --p { font-family: F; override-colors: 0 red } with font-palette: --p
- `feat:css-fonts-4/@font-palette-values/font-family` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#descdef-font-palette-values-font-family): minimal `<text>` case exercising: Palette rule descriptor `font-family`.
- `feat:css-fonts-4/@font-palette-values/base-palette` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#descdef-font-palette-values-base-palette): minimal `<text>` case exercising: Palette rule descriptor `base-palette`.
- `feat:css-fonts-4/@font-palette-values/override-colors` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#descdef-font-palette-values-override-colors): minimal `<text>` case exercising: Palette rule descriptor `override-colors`.
- `feat:css-fonts-4/cluster-matching-variation-selectors` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#cluster-matching): U+2764 with VS15 and VS16 where both a text face and a color emoji face are registered
- `feat:css-fonts-4/feature-precedence` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#feature-variation-precedence): minimal `<text>` case exercising: Feature settings resolve in a fixed order: defaults, `@font-face` descriptors, font-variant, font-feature-settin
- `feat:css-fonts-4/language-specific-display` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-fonts-4-20261112/#language-specific-support): Same Cyrillic text under xml:lang=sr and xml:lang=ru in a face with locl alternates
- `feat:font-format/collections` (https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/typography/opentype/spec/otff#font-collections): `@font-face` src: url(fonts.ttc#2): the second face must be used
- `feat:font-format/colr-v0` (https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/typography/opentype/spec/colr): COLRv0 test face: layers must paint with palette colors, not the fill
- `feat:font-format/colr-v1` (https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/typography/opentype/spec/colr): COLRv1 gradient glyph
- `feat:font-format/svg-table` (https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/typography/opentype/spec/svg): OpenType-SVG face with a glyph using context-fill
- `feat:font-format/sbix` (https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/typography/opentype/spec/sbix): sbix test face on the full backend with a trusted font
- `feat:font-format/legacy-sfnt-magic` (https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/typography/opentype/spec/otff): A TrueType font whose version tag is 'true' must load
- `feat:woff1/reserved-field` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2012/REC-WOFF-20121213/#conform-reserved-reject): WOFF with reserved = 1 must not load
- `feat:woff1/totalsfntsize` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2012/REC-WOFF-20121213/#conform-totalsize-longword-reject): WOFF with totalSfntSize off by 2 must not load
- `feat:woff1/overlap-and-extraneous-data` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2012/REC-WOFF-20121213/#conform-extraneous-reject): WOFF with two directory entries sharing one byte range must not load
- `feat:woff1/complength-gt-origlength` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2012/REC-WOFF-20121213/#conform-compLength): WOFF table with compLength > origLength holding a valid zlib stream
- `feat:woff1/metadata-and-private-blocks` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2012/REC-WOFF-20121213/#conform-metadata-noeffect): WOFF whose metadata block overlaps a table must not load
- `feat:woff1/ascending-directory` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2012/REC-WOFF-20121213/#conform-ascending-recreated): WOFF with reversed directory order: reconstructed sfnt must be sorted
- `feat:woff1/private-font-scope` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2012/REC-WOFF-20121213/#conform-css3font-available): minimal `<text>` case exercising: Linked fonts are available only to the documents that reference them.
- `feat:woff2/totalsfntsize-mismatch` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2024/REC-WOFF2-20240808/#conform-mustNotRejectIncorrectTotalSize): WOFF2 whose totalSfntSize is 4 bytes larger than the decoded font must still load
- `val:text-transform/full-size-kana` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CRD-css-text-3-20260814/#valdef-text-transform-full-size-kana): <text style="text-transform: full-size-kana"> with small kana
- `val:text-transform/<case> || full-width || full-size-kana` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CRD-css-text-3-20260814/#propdef-text-transform): text-transform: uppercase full-width
- `feat:css-text-3/segment-break-transformation` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CRD-css-text-3-20260814/#line-break-transform): `<text>` with two CJK ideographs separated by a newline: no gap may appear
- `prop:tab-size` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CRD-css-text-3-20260814/#propdef-tab-size): white-space: pre with tab-size: 4 must halve the tab stop distance
- `val:tab-size/<number [0,inf]>` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CRD-css-text-3-20260814/#propdef-tab-size): <text style="tab-size: <number [0,inf]>"> compared with a reference
- `val:tab-size/<length [0,inf]>` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CRD-css-text-3-20260814/#propdef-tab-size): <text style="tab-size: <length [0,inf]>"> compared with a reference

- `val:word-break/normal` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CRD-css-text-3-20260814/#valdef-word-break-normal): <text style="word-break: normal"> compared with a reference

- `val:text-emphasis-position/over` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2022/CRD-css-text-decor-3-20220505/#valdef-text-emphasis-position-over): <text style="text-emphasis-position: over"> compared with a reference

- `val:text-emphasis-position/right` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2022/CRD-css-text-decor-3-20220505/#valdef-text-emphasis-position-right): <text style="text-emphasis-position: right"> compared with a reference
- `val:text-emphasis-position/left` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2022/CRD-css-text-decor-3-20220505/#valdef-text-emphasis-position-left): <text style="text-emphasis-position: left"> compared with a reference

- `prop:text-shadow` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2022/CRD-css-text-decor-3-20220505/#propdef-text-shadow): <text style="text-shadow: 2px 2px 3px red">
- `val:text-shadow/none` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2022/CRD-css-text-decor-3-20220505/#propdef-text-shadow): <text style="text-shadow: none"> compared with a reference

- `prop:white-space-collapse` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-text-4-20260814/#propdef-white-space-collapse): <text style="white-space-collapse: preserve-spaces"> must equal xml:space="preserve"
- `val:white-space-collapse/collapse` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-text-4-20260814/#valdef-white-space-collapse-collapse): <text style="white-space-collapse: collapse"> compared with a reference

- `val:white-space-collapse/preserve` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-text-4-20260814/#valdef-white-space-collapse-preserve): <text style="white-space-collapse: preserve"> compared with a reference
- `val:white-space-collapse/preserve-breaks` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-text-4-20260814/#valdef-white-space-collapse-preserve-breaks): <text style="white-space-collapse: preserve-breaks"> compared with a reference
- `val:white-space-collapse/preserve-spaces` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-text-4-20260814/#valdef-white-space-collapse-preserve-spaces): <text style="white-space-collapse: preserve-spaces"> compared with a reference
- `val:white-space-collapse/break-spaces` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-text-4-20260814/#valdef-white-space-collapse-break-spaces): <text style="white-space-collapse: break-spaces"> compared with a reference
- `prop:text-wrap-mode` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-text-4-20260814/#propdef-text-wrap-mode): <text style="text-wrap-mode: nowrap; inline-size: 50px"> must not wrap
- `val:text-wrap-mode/wrap` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-text-4-20260814/#valdef-text-wrap-mode-wrap): <text style="text-wrap-mode: wrap"> compared with a reference
- `val:text-wrap-mode/nowrap` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-text-4-20260814/#valdef-text-wrap-mode-nowrap): <text style="text-wrap-mode: nowrap"> compared with a reference
- `prop:white-space-trim` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-text-4-20260814/#propdef-white-space-trim): <tspan style="white-space-trim: discard-before"> after a space
- `val:white-space-trim/none` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-text-4-20260814/#propdef-white-space-trim): <text style="white-space-trim: none"> compared with a reference
- `val:white-space-trim/discard-before` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-text-4-20260814/#valdef-white-space-trim-discard-before): <text style="white-space-trim: discard-before"> compared with a reference
- `val:white-space-trim/discard-after` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-text-4-20260814/#valdef-white-space-trim-discard-after): <text style="white-space-trim: discard-after"> compared with a reference
- `val:white-space-trim/discard-inner` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-text-4-20260814/#valdef-white-space-trim-discard-inner): <text style="white-space-trim: discard-inner"> compared with a reference
- `val:writing-mode/sideways-rl` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#valdef-writing-mode-sideways-rl): <text style="writing-mode: sideways-rl">
- `val:writing-mode/sideways-lr` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#valdef-writing-mode-sideways-lr): <text style="writing-mode: sideways-lr">
- `val:text-orientation/sideways-right` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#valdef-text-orientation-sideways-right): text-orientation: sideways-right must equal sideways
- `prop:text-combine-upright` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#propdef-text-combine-upright): vertical text with <tspan style='text-combine-upright: all'>12</tspan>
- `val:text-combine-upright/none` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#valdef-text-combine-upright-none): <text style="text-combine-upright: none"> compared with a reference
- `val:text-combine-upright/all` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#valdef-text-combine-upright-all): <text style="text-combine-upright: all"> compared with a reference
- `val:text-combine-upright/digits <integer>?` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2019/CR-css-writing-modes-4-20190730/#valdef-text-combine-upright-digits-integer): <text style="text-combine-upright: digits `<integer>`?"> compared with a reference
- `val:baseline-shift/top` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2024/WD-css-inline-3-20241218/#valdef-baseline-shift-top): <tspan style="baseline-shift: top"> in a two-size line
- `val:baseline-shift/center` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2024/WD-css-inline-3-20241218/#valdef-baseline-shift-center): <tspan style="baseline-shift: center"> in a two-size line
- `val:baseline-shift/bottom` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2024/WD-css-inline-3-20241218/#valdef-baseline-shift-bottom): <tspan style="baseline-shift: bottom"> in a two-size line
- `prop:vertical-align` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2024/WD-css-inline-3-20241218/#propdef-vertical-align): <tspan style="vertical-align: super"> must match baseline-shift: super
- `val:vertical-align/first | last` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2024/WD-css-inline-3-20241218/#propdef-vertical-align): <text style="vertical-align: first | last"> compared with a reference
- `val:vertical-align/<'alignment-baseline'>` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2024/WD-css-inline-3-20241218/#propdef-vertical-align): <text style="vertical-align: <'alignment-baseline'>"> compared with a reference
- `val:vertical-align/<'baseline-shift'>` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2024/WD-css-inline-3-20241218/#propdef-vertical-align): <text style="vertical-align: <'baseline-shift'>"> compared with a reference
- `val:line-height/normal` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2024/WD-css-inline-3-20241218/#valdef-line-height-normal): inline-size text with line-height: normal
- `val:line-height/<number [0,inf]>` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2024/WD-css-inline-3-20241218/#valdef-line-height-number-0): inline-size text with line-height: <number [0,inf]>
- `val:line-height/<length [0,inf]>` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2024/WD-css-inline-3-20241218/#valdef-line-height-length-0): inline-size text with line-height: <length [0,inf]>
- `val:line-height/<percentage [0,inf]>` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2024/WD-css-inline-3-20241218/#valdef-line-height-percentage-0): inline-size text with line-height: <percentage [0,inf]>
- `prop:line-fit-edge` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2024/WD-css-inline-3-20241218/#propdef-line-fit-edge): wrapped text with line-fit-edge: cap alphabetic
- `val:line-fit-edge/leading` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2024/WD-css-inline-3-20241218/#propdef-line-fit-edge): <text style="line-fit-edge: leading"> compared with a reference
- `val:line-fit-edge/<text-edge>` (https://www.w3.org/TR/2024/WD-css-inline-3-20241218/#typedef-text-edge): <text style="line-fit-edge: `<text-edge>`"> compared with a reference

## Unverified / could not fetch

All W3C and Unicode pages listed under "Specs read" were fetched. The OpenType
specification pages were not fetched; OpenType anchors identify the table only, and no
requirement text was taken from them.

Statements marked unverified in the matrix:

- `feat:font-format/cff2`: that the basic backend cannot decode CFF2 rests on stb_truetype's
  known feature set, not on a Donner test.
- `feat:font-format/variable-fonts`: that document `@font-face` variable fonts render at the
  default instance follows from the provider-request guard; no test covers a document face.
- `feat:font-format/sbix`: no sbix font or test exists; support depends on the FreeType build.
- `feat:font-format/collections` and `feat:woff2/collections`: the fate of a decoded WOFF2
  collection after the preflight was not traced to a test.
- `feat:woff1/complength-gt-origlength`: rejection depends on the inflate routine's result.
- `feat:css-fonts-4/cluster-matching-variation-selectors`: absence shown by one grep of the
  layout engine only.
- `val:letter-spacing/<length>`: the end-of-line behavior is read from the pen-advance code;
  no test asserts the trailing space either way.
- `val:font-family/escaped-identifier`: escape decoding is the CSS tokenizer's responsibility
  and was not audited here.
- Line numbers were taken from the pinned revision; ranges are the enclosing function or
  branch, not always the single deciding line.
